# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-10-10 through 2026-10-16

## Best date plans

### 2026-10-11 — Pioneer Works Second Sundays + Masalawala & Sons

**Pioneer Works Second Sundays, then a meal.** Bengali and Indian at Masalawala & Sons. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=159+Pioneer+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&destination=365+Fifth+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://pioneerworks.org/programs/second-sundays-october-2026) · [Restaurant](https://www.masalawala.com/)

### 2026-10-11 — Cozy and Crafted Bazaar + Tatiana

**Cozy and Crafted Bazaar, then a meal.** Afro-Caribbean American at Tatiana. It is a 14-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=100+West+77th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10024&destination=10+Lincoln+Center+Plaza%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://grandbazaarnyc.org/events/cozy-and-crafted-bazaar/) · [Restaurant](https://www.tatiananyc.com/)

### 2026-10-10 — Red Hook Open Studios + Rucola

[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Red+Hook+Open+Studios&destination=190+Dean+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.redhookopenstudios.com/) · [Restaurant](https://www.rucolabrooklyn.com/)

**Dinner, then Max ZT, Kunal Gunjal, and Amit Kavthekar: Strings of Soul.** Bengali and Indian at Masalawala & Sons. It is a 22-minute walk from the venue. Max ZT, Kunal Gunjal, and Amit Kavthekar: Strings of Soul starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.

#### [New York African Restaurant Week Festival](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/2026-new-york-african-restaurant-week-festival-oct-9-11-tickets-1986132308199)

12:00 PM · food · Free · Restoration Plaza · Bed-Stuy · Brooklyn · Score 74.0  
Free three-day festival with more than 40 marketplace vendors, food from African and Caribbean cuisines, DJs, live cultural performances, a fashion show, and a Sunday jollof tasting contest.
**Timing:** Festival runs October 9-11; The Skint lists noon-7pm daily.
**Practical note:** Sunday has a 90% NWS rain chance; bring rain gear and confirm any outdoor programming before going.
**Why now:** The in-person festival weekend falls inside the window, October 9-11.
**On-sale watch:** Food vouchers are while supplies last.

#### [Queens Night Market](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

4:00 PM · markets · Free entry; most dishes capped at a few dollars · Queens Night Market · Corona · Queens · Score 56.0  
Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.
**Timing:** Saturdays 4pm to midnight. Note the seasonal gap between late August and mid September.
**Practical note:** Outdoors and cash-friendly; the adjacent lot charges for parking, so the 7 train is easier.
**Why now:** Runs every Saturday evening in season; the 7 train to 111th St drops you four blocks away.

## Where to eat

Verified against captured public evidence for the 2026-10-10 NYC dining guide; keep the dated specials and substantiated recent openings, but collapse true duplicates and relabel ordinary menus as restaurant picks.

Dining research: 2026-10-10T10:36:35+00:00

No candidate restaurant was verified as newly Michelin-starred. The only Michelin-adjacent public-source fact found was Resy’s background reference to Eleven Madison Park as Daniel Humm’s three-Michelin-starred restaurant and Eater’s background reference to El Califa de León’s Mexico City original as the first Michelin-starred taqueria in 2024; do not convert those into awards for Humm or the NYC El Califa listing.

### Good Time Country Buffet’s under-$30 Southern buffet

Deals & happy hours — Good Time Country Buffet

The Kisa/C as in Charlie team opened a throwback Southern country buffet with fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, rotating specials, salad bar, and separate desserts/drinks.

Price: Adults $26.99; seniors 65+ $23.99; kids 4-9 $13.99; drinks and desserts extra  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday 5:00 PM-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Eater verifies $26.99 adults, $23.99 ages 65+, $13.99 kids 4-9, desserts and boozy peach frozen drinks separate, Wednesday-Sunday 5-10 p.m., walk-ins plus limited Resy reservations: https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa  
From: 2026-09-12  
Opened: 2026-09-12  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa)

### Lunch prix-fixe menu availability at a downtown fine-dining room

Special menus — Crown Shy

Official menu page states a lunch prix-fixe menu is available alongside a la carte lunch items such as chicken kofta, salmon, pork katsu sandwich, and tandoori flatbreads.

Price: Prix-fixe price not exposed; official lunch a la carte examples run $20-$49, with dessert to go at $8  
Schedule: Lunch; exact days/hours not visible in fetched menu markup  
Conditions: Official menu capture states “Lunch Menu / Prix Fixe Menu Available” but does not expose prix-fixe courses or price; tax, tip, service charges and exact lunch days were not verified: https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/  
[Source](https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/)

### Christmas Eve Feast of the Seven Fishes

Special menus — Union Square Cafe

Union Square Cafe lists its annual Christmas Eve Feast of the Seven Fishes with family-style antipasti, pasta choices, entree choices, and dessert.

Price: Four courses $205 per person; three courses $63 for children  
Schedule: Christmas Eve; exact seating times not shown in fetched menu markup  
Conditions: Official menu capture verifies four courses at $205 per person and three courses at $63 for children; beverage, tax and gratuity additional, with seasonal changes expected: https://www.unionsquarecafe.com/menus/  
Event date: 2026-12-24  
[Source](https://www.unionsquarecafe.com/menus/)

### New Year's Eve multi-course menu

Special menus — Union Square Cafe

Official menu lists a celebratory New Year's Eve multi-course format with appetizer, midcourse, entree, and dessert choices.

Price: $185 per person  
Schedule: New Year's Eve; exact seating times not shown in fetched menu markup  
Conditions: Official menu capture verifies the New Year’s Eve celebratory multi-course menu at $185 per person; seating times, beverages, tax, gratuity and cancellation terms were not exposed: https://www.unionsquarecafe.com/menus/  
Event date: 2026-12-31  
[Source](https://www.unionsquarecafe.com/menus/)

### Thanksgiving prix-fixe-style holiday menu

Special menus — Union Square Cafe

Official menu lists a Thanksgiving format with appetizer, entree, shared sides, and dessert choices, including turkey, pumpkin lasagna, swordfish piccata, and beef tenderloin.

Price: $185 per guest; $65 two-course children’s menu with cookies  
Schedule: Thanksgiving Day; exact times not shown in fetched menu markup  
Conditions: Official Thanksgiving capture states price is inclusive of the listed menu, while beverage, tax and gratuity are additional and seasonal menu changes should be expected: https://www.unionsquarecafe.com/menus/  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
[Source](https://www.unionsquarecafe.com/menus/)

### Daniel Humm's casual West Village restaurant opened with wood-fire cooking

New openings — Humm

Resy reports Daniel Humm's more casual, a la carte restaurant opening with Dmitri Magi leading the kitchen, wood-fire and charcoal-grilled dishes, caviar bagel, branzino, roasted chicken for two, and vegetable-focused plates.

Schedule: Open Sunday-Thursday 5:00 PM-10:00 PM and Friday-Saturday 5:00 PM-10:30 PM  
Conditions: Resy verifies opening October 10, à la carte modern American wood-fire menu, reservations dropping three weeks out at noon, and some walk-in availability especially at the bar; public article did not expose full prices, service charges or cancellation terms: https://blog.resy.com/2026/10/humm-nyc/  
Opened: 2026-10-10  
Published: 2026-10-06  
[Source](https://blog.resy.com/2026/10/humm-nyc/)

### Daniel Boulud opened a Lincoln Center brasserie

New openings — Brasserie Boulud

Resy reports Boulud's classic French brasserie opening near Lincoln Center with chef Christina D'Angelo, French classics, pastas, rotisserie, steak frites, côte de boeuf, and limited Original DB burger.

Schedule: Open Tuesday-Sunday 4:30 PM-10:30 PM; hours expected to expand later  
Conditions: Resy verifies Brasserie Boulud was set to open October 8 near Lincoln Center at the corner of 64th and Broadway, with reservations dropping 30 days in advance at midnight; prices and service/cancellation terms were not exposed: https://blog.resy.com/2026/10/brasserie-boulud-nyc/  
Opened: 2026-10-08  
Published: 2026-10-05  
[Source](https://blog.resy.com/2026/10/brasserie-boulud-nyc/)

### Iara’s Brazilian Amazonian cooking on the Lower East Side

New openings — Iara

Resy reports chef Vitor Mendes’s Iara, opening October 2, with Brazilian and Amazonian cooking including moqueca, pão de queijo with smoky eggplant dip, picanha, tapioca-and-tuna, tucupi cocktails, caipirinhas and most dishes intended to stay below $20.

Price: Most dishes below $20 per Resy interview; exact menu prices unknown  
Schedule: Tuesday-Saturday 5:00 PM-11:00 PM  
Conditions: Reservations drop 14 days in advance at noon and walk-ins are welcome per Resy; exact address, itemized menu prices, tax/tip and service charges were not exposed in the captured source: https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/iara-nyc/  
Opened: 2026-10-02  
Published: 2026-09-25  
[Source](https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/iara-nyc/)

### El Camarón Peludo’s Mexican seafood-and-wine bar in the West Village

New openings — El Camarón Peludo

Eater’s October Manhattan heatmap describes the September opening from the Taqueria Ramirez/Carnitas Ramirez team as a small Mexican seafood-and-wine bar with sardine tacos and michelada-dressed oysters.

Conditions: Eater verifies address, September opening context and the restaurant’s heatmap inclusion; hours, prices, tax/tip and service charges were not exposed: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap  
Published: 2026-10-01  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap)

### Mythily’s Tamil and South Indian restaurant in Williamsburg

New openings — Mythily

Eater verifies Top Chef alum Rasika Venkatesa turned Mythily from a pop-up into a September Williamsburg restaurant focused on Tamil and South Indian cooking, with dishes such as scallop crudo with Concord grapes and puli inji, slow-roasted cabbage and rasmalai-tres leches.
